goal of \u003ccode\u003ellama.cpp\u003c/code\u003e is to enable LLM inference with minimal setup and state-of-the-art performance on a wide\nrange of hardware - locally and in the cloud.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePlain C/C++ implementation without any dependencies\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eApple silicon is a first-class citizen - optimized via ARM NEON, Accelerate and Metal frameworks\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAVX, AVX2, AVX512 and AMX support for x86 architectures\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRVV, ZVFH, ZFH, ZICBOP and ZIHINTPAUSE support for RISC-V architectures\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e1.5-bit, 2-bit, 3-bit, 4-bit, 5-bit, 6-bit, and 8-bit integer quantization for faster inference and reduced memory use\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCustom CUDA kernels for running LLMs on NVIDIA GPUs (support for AMD GPUs via HIP and Moore Threads GPUs via MUSA)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVulkan and SYCL backend support\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCPU+GPU hybrid inference to partially accelerate models larger than the total VRAM capacity\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003ellama.cpp\u003c/code\u003e project is the main playground for developing new features for the \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml\"\u003eggml\u003c/a\u003e library.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdetails\u003e\n\u003csummary\u003eModels\u003c/summary\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTypically finetunes of the base models below are supported as well.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInstructions for adding support for new models: \u003ca directory, enabling sharing with other HF tools.**","- **[guide : using the new WebUI of llama.cpp](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/16938)**","- [guide : running gpt-oss with llama.cpp](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/15396)","- [[FEEDBACK] Better packaging for llama.cpp to support downstream consumers 🤗](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/15313)","- Support for the `gpt-oss` model with native MXFP4 format has been added | [PR](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/15091) | [Collaboration with NVIDIA](https://blogs.nvidia.com/blog/rtx-ai-garage-openai-oss) | [Comment](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/15095)","- Multimodal support arrived in `llama-server`: [#12898](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/12898) | [documentation](./docs/multimodal.md)","- VS Code extension for FIM completions: https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.vscode","- Vim/Neovim plugin for FIM completions: https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.vim","- Hugging Face Inference Endpoints now support GGUF out of the box! https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9669","- Hugging Face GGUF editor: [discussion](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9268) | [tool](https://huggingface.co/spaces/CISCai/gguf-editor)","- WebGPU support is now available in the browser, see a blog/demo introducing it [here](https://reeselevine.github.io/llamas-on-the-web/).","","----","","## Quick start","","Getting started with llama.cpp is straightforward. Here are several ways to install it on your machine:","","- Install `llama.cpp` using [brew, nix, winget, or conda-forge](docs/install.md)","- Run with Docker - see our [Docker documentation](docs/docker.md)","- Download pre-built binaries from the [releases page](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/releases)","- Build from source by cloning this repository - check out [our build guide](docs/build.md)","","Once installed, you'll need a model to work with. Head to the [Obtaining and quantizing models](#obtaining-and-quantizing-models) section to learn more.","","Example command:","","```sh","# Use a local model file","llama-cli -m my_model.gguf","","# Or download and run a model directly from Hugging Face","llama-cli -hf ggml-org/gemma-3-1b-it-GGUF","","# Launch OpenAI-compatible API server","llama-server -hf ggml-org/gemma-3-1b-it-GGUF","```","","## Description","","The main goal of `llama.cpp` is to enable LLM inference with minimal setup and state-of-the-art performance on a wide","range of hardware - locally and in the cloud.","","- Plain C/C++ implementation without any dependencies","- Apple silicon is a first-class citizen - optimized via ARM NEON, Accelerate and Metal frameworks","- AVX, AVX2, AVX512 and AMX support for x86 architectures","- RVV, ZVFH, ZFH, ZICBOP and ZIHINTPAUSE support for RISC-V architectures","- 1.5-bit, 2-bit, 3-bit, 4-bit, 5-bit, 6-bit, and 8-bit integer quantization for faster inference and reduced memory use","- Custom CUDA kernels for running LLMs on NVIDIA GPUs (support for AMD GPUs via HIP and Moore Threads GPUs via MUSA)","- Vulkan and SYCL backend support","- CPU+GPU hybrid inference to partially accelerate models larger than the total VRAM capacity","","The `llama.cpp` project is the main playground for developing new features for the [ggml](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml) library.","","\u003cdetails\u003e","\u003csummary\u003eModels\u003c/summary\u003e","","Typically finetunes of the base models below are supported as well.","","Instructions for adding support for new models: [HOWTO-add-model.md](docs/development/HOWTO-add-model.md)","","#### Text-only","","- [X] LLaMA 🦙","- [x] LLaMA 2 🦙🦙","- [x] LLaMA 3 🦙🦙🦙","- where agents controlled by an AI model will try to trick you.","","\u003c/details\u003e","","","## Supported backends","","| Backend | Target devices |","| --- | --- |","| [Metal](docs/build.md#metal-build) | Apple Silicon |","| [BLAS](docs/build.md#blas-build) | All |","| [BLIS](docs/backend/BLIS.md) | All |","| [SYCL](docs/backend/SYCL.md) | Intel GPU |","| [OpenVINO [In Progress]](docs/backend/OPENVINO.md) | Intel CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs |","| [MUSA](docs/build.md#musa) | Moore Threads GPU |","| [CUDA](docs/build.md#cuda) | Nvidia GPU |","| [HIP](docs/build.md#hip) | AMD GPU |","| [ZenDNN](docs/build.md#zendnn) | AMD CPU |","| [Vulkan](docs/build.md#vulkan) | GPU |","| [CANN](docs/build.md#cann) | Ascend NPU |","| [OpenCL](docs/backend/OPENCL.md) | Adreno GPU |","| [IBM zDNN](docs/backend/zDNN.md) | IBM Z \u0026 LinuxONE |","| [WebGPU](docs/build.md#webgpu) | All |","| [RPC](https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/tree/master/tools/rpc) | All |","| [Hexagon [In Progress]](docs/backend/snapdragon/README.md) | Snapdragon |","| [VirtGPU](docs/backend/VirtGPU.md) | VirtGPU APIR |","","## Obtaining and quantizing models","","The [Hugging Face](https://huggingface.co) platform hosts a [number of LLMs](https://huggingface.co/models?library=gguf\u0026sort=trending) compatible with `llama.cpp`:","","- [Trending](https://huggingface.co/models?library=gguf\u0026sort=trending)","- [LLaMA](https://huggingface.co/models?sort=trending\u0026search=llama+gguf)","","You can either manually download the GGUF file or directly use any `llama.cpp`-compatible models from [Hugging Face](https://huggingface.co/) or other model hosting sites, by using this CLI argument: `-hf \u003cuser\u003e/\u003cmodel\u003e[:quant]`. For example:","","```sh","llama-cli -hf ggml-org/gemma-3-1b-it-GGUF","```","","By default, the CLI would download from Hugging Face, you can switch to other options with the environment variable `MODEL_ENDPOINT`. The `MODEL_ENDPOINT` must point to a Hugging Face compatible API endpoint.","","After downloading a model, use the CLI tools to run it locally - see below.","","`llama.cpp` requires the model to be stored in the [GGUF](https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/blob/master/docs/gguf.md) file format. Models in other data formats can be converted to GGUF using the `convert_*.py` Python scripts in this repo.","","The Hugging Face platform provides a variety of online tools for converting, quantizing and hosting models with `llama.cpp`:","","- Use the [GGUF-my-repo space](https://huggingface.co/spaces/ggml-org/gguf-my-repo) to convert to GGUF format and quantize model weights to smaller sizes","- Use the [GGUF-my-LoRA space](https://huggingface.co/spaces/ggml-org/gguf-my-lora) to convert LoRA adapters to GGUF format (more info: https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/10123)","- Use the [GGUF-editor space](https://huggingface.co/spaces/CISCai/gguf-editor) to edit GGUF meta data in the browser (more info: https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9268)","- Use the [Inference Endpoints](https://ui.endpoints.huggingface.co/) to directly host `llama.cpp` in the cloud (more info: https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9669)","","To learn more about model quantization, [read this documentation](tools/quantize/README.md)","","## [`llama-cli`](tools/cli)","","#### A CLI tool for accessing and experimenting with most of `llama.cpp`'s functionality.","","- \u003cdetails open\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eRun in conversation mode\u003c/summary\u003e","","    Models with a built-in chat template will automatically activate conversation mode. If this doesn't occur, you can manually enable it by adding `-cnv` and specifying a suitable chat template with `--chat-template NAME`","","    ```bash","    llama-cli -m model.gguf","","    # \u003e hi, who are you?","    # Hi there! I'm your helpful assistant! I'm an AI-powered chatbot designed to assist and provide information to users like you. I'm here to help answer your questions, provide guidance, and offer support on a wide range of topics. I'm a friendly and knowledgeable AI, and I'm always happy to help with anything you need. What's on your mind, and how can I assist you today?","    #","    # \u003e what is 1+1?","    # Easy peasy! The answer to 1+1 is... 2!","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","- \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eRun in conversation mode with custom chat template\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    # use the \"chatml\" template (use -h to see the list of supported templates)","    llama-cli -m model.gguf -cnv --chat-template chatml","","    # use a custom template","    llama-cli -m model.gguf -cnv --in-prefix 'User: ' --reverse-prompt 'User:'","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","- \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eConstrain the output with a custom grammar\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    llama-cli -m model.gguf -n 256 --grammar-file grammars/json.gbnf -p 'Request: schedule a call at 8pm; Command:'","","    # {\"appointmentTime\": \"8pm\", \"appointmentDetails\": \"schedule a a call\"}","    ```","","    The [grammars/](grammars/) folder contains a handful of sample grammars. To write your own, check out the [GBNF Guide](grammars/README.md).","","    For authoring more complex JSON grammars, check out https://grammar.intrinsiclabs.ai/","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","","## [`llama-server`](tools/server)","","#### A lightweight, [OpenAI API](https://github.com/openai/openai-openapi) compatible, HTTP server for serving LLMs.","","- \u003cdetails open\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eStart a local HTTP server with default configuration on port 8080\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    llama-server -m model.gguf --port 8080","","    # Basic web UI can be accessed via browser: http://localhost:8080","    # Chat completion endpoint: http://localhost:8080/v1/chat/completions","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","- \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eSupport multiple-users and parallel decoding\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    # up to 4 concurrent requests, each with 4096 max context","    llama-server -m model.gguf -c 16384 -np 4","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","- \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eEnable speculative decoding\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    # the draft.gguf model should be a small variant of the target model.gguf","    llama-server -m model.gguf -md draft.gguf","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","- \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eServe an embedding model\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    # use the /embedding endpoint","    llama-server -m model.gguf --embedding --pooling cls -ub 8192","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","- \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eServe a reranking model\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    # use the /reranking endpoint","    llama-server -m model.gguf --reranking","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","- \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eConstrain all outputs with a grammar\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    # custom grammar","    llama-server -m model.gguf --grammar-file grammar.gbnf","","    # JSON","    llama-server -m model.gguf --grammar-file grammars/json.gbnf","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","","## [`llama-perplexity`](tools/perplexity)","","#### A tool for measuring the [perplexity](tools/perplexity/README.md) [^1] (and other quality metrics) of a model over a given text.","","- \u003cdetails open\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eMeasure the perplexity over a text file\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    llama-perplexity -m model.gguf -f file.txt","","    # [1]15.2701,[2]5.4007,[3]5.3073,[4]6.2965,[5]5.8940,[6]5.6096,[7]5.7942,[8]4.9297, ...","    # Final estimate: PPL = 5.4007 +/- 0.67339","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","- \u003cdetails\u003e","    \u003csummary\u003eMeasure KL divergence\u003c/summary\u003e","","    ```bash","    # build: 3e0ba0e60 (4229)","    ```","","    \u003c/details\u003e","","## [`llama-simple`](examples/simple)","","#### A minimal example for implementing apps with `llama.cpp`. This is especially important when choosing an appropriate model size and appreciating both the significant and subtle differences between LLaMA models and ChatGPT:","- LLaMA:","    - [Introducing LLaMA: A foundational, 65-billion-parameter large language model](https://ai.facebook.com/blog/large-language-model-llama-meta-ai/)","    - [LLaMA: Open and Efficient Foundation Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.13971)","- GPT-3","    - [Language Models are Few-Shot Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2005.14165)","- GPT-3.5 / InstructGPT / ChatGPT:","    - [Aligning language models to follow instructions](https://openai.com/research/instruction-following)","    - [Training language models to follow instructions with human feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.02155)","","## XCFramework","The XCFramework is a precompiled version of the library for iOS, visionOS, tvOS,","and macOS. It can be used in Swift projects without the need to compile the","library from source. For example:","```swift","// swift-tools-version: 5.10","// The swift-tools-version declares the minimum version of Swift required to build this package.","","import PackageDescription","","let package = Package(","    name: \"MyLlamaPackage\",","    targets: [","        .executableTarget(","            name: \"MyLlamaPackage\",","            dependencies: [","                \"LlamaFramework\"","            ]),","        .binaryTarget(","            name: \"LlamaFramework\",","            url: \"https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/releases/download/b5046/llama-b5046-xcframework.zip\",","            checksum: \"c19be78b5f00d8d29a25da41042cb7afa094cbf6280a225abe614b03b20029ab\"","        )","    ]",")","```","The above example is using an intermediate build `b5046` of the library. This can be modified","to use a different version by changing the URL and checksum.","","## Completions","Command-line completion is available for some environments.","","#### Bash Completion","```bash","$ build/bin/llama-cli --completion-bash \u003e ~/.llama-completion.bash","$ source ~/.llama-completion.bash","```","Optionally this can be added to your `.bashrc` or `.bash_profile` to load it","automatically. <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Getting started with llama.cpp is straightforward. Here are several ways to install it on your machine:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Install <code>llama.cpp</code> using <a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/docs/install.md">brew, nix, winget, or conda-forge</a></li>
<li>Run with Docker - see our <a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/docs/docker.md">Docker documentation</a></li>
<li>Download pre-built binaries from the <a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/releases">releases page</a></li>
<li>Build from source by cloning this repository - check out <a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/docs/build.md">our build guide</a></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Once installed, you'll need a model to work with. Head to the <a href="#obtaining-and-quantizing-models">Obtaining and quantizing models</a> section to learn more.</p>
<p dir="auto">Example command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Use a local model file
llama-cli -m my_model.gguf

# Or download and run a model directly from Hugging Face
llama-cli -hf ggml-org/gemma-3-1b-it-GGUF

# Launch OpenAI-compatible API server
llama-server -hf ggml-org/gemma-3-1b-it-GGUF"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Use a local model file</span>
llama-cli -m my_model.gguf

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or download and run a model directly from Hugging Face</span>
llama-cli -hf ggml-org/gemma-3-1b-it-GGUF

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Launch OpenAI-compatible API server</span>
llama-server -hf ggml-org/gemma-3-1b-it-GGUF</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Description</h2><a id="user-content-description" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Description" href="#description"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The main goal of <code>llama.cpp</code> is to enable LLM inference with minimal setup and state-of-the-art performance on a wide
range of hardware - locally and in the cloud.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Plain C/C++ implementation without any dependencies</li>
<li>Apple silicon is a first-class citizen - optimized via ARM NEON, Accelerate and Metal frameworks</li>
<li>AVX, AVX2, AVX512 and AMX support for x86 architectures</li>
<li>RVV, ZVFH, ZFH, ZICBOP and ZIHINTPAUSE support for RISC-V architectures</li>
<li>1.5-bit, 2-bit, 3-bit, 4-bit, 5-bit, 6-bit, and 8-bit integer quantization for faster inference and reduced memory use</li>
<li>Custom CUDA kernels for running LLMs on NVIDIA GPUs (support for AMD GPUs via HIP and Moore Threads GPUs via MUSA)</li>
<li>Vulkan and SYCL backend support</li>
<li>CPU+GPU hybrid inference to partially accelerate models larger than the total VRAM capacity</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">The <code>llama.cpp</code> project is the main playground for developing new features for the <a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml">ggml</a> library.</p>
<details>
<summary>Models</summary>
<p dir="auto">Typically finetunes of the base models below are supported as well.</p>
<p dir="auto">Instructions for adding support for new models: <a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/docs/development/HOWTO-add-model.md">HOWTO-add-model.md</a></p>

<summary>Tools</summary>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/akx/ggify">akx/ggify</a> – download PyTorch models from Hugging Face Hub and convert them to GGML</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/akx/ollama-dl">akx/ollama-dl</a> – download models from the Ollama library to be used directly with llama.cpp</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/crashr/gppm">crashr/gppm</a> – launch llama.cpp instances utilizing NVIDIA Tesla P40 or P100 GPUs with reduced idle power consumption</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/gpustack/gguf-parser-go/tree/main/cmd/gguf-parser">gpustack/gguf-parser</a> - review/check the GGUF file and estimate the memory usage</li>
<li><a href="https://marketplace.unity.com/packages/tools/generative-ai/styled-lines-llama-cpp-model-292902" rel="nofollow">Styled Lines</a> (proprietary licensed, async wrapper of inference part for game development in Unity3d with pre-built Mobile and Web platform wrappers and a model example)</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/unslothai/unsloth">unslothai/unsloth</a> – 🦥 exports/saves fine-tuned and trained models to GGUF (Apache-2.0)</li>
</ul>

support"</li>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Obtaining and quantizing models</h2><a id="user-content-obtaining-and-quantizing-models" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Obtaining and quantizing models" href="#obtaining-and-quantizing-models"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <a href="https://huggingface.co" rel="nofollow">Hugging Face</a> platform hosts a <a href="https://huggingface.co/models?library=gguf&amp;sort=trending" rel="nofollow">number of LLMs</a> compatible with <code>llama.cpp</code>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://huggingface.co/models?library=gguf&amp;sort=trending" rel="nofollow">Trending</a></li>
<li><a href="https://huggingface.co/models?sort=trending&amp;search=llama+gguf" rel="nofollow">LLaMA</a></li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">You can either manually download the GGUF file or directly use any <code>llama.cpp</code>-compatible models from <a href="https://huggingface.co/" rel="nofollow">Hugging Face</a> or other model hosting sites, by using this CLI argument: <code>-hf &lt;user&gt;/&lt;model&gt;[:quant]</code>. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="llama-cli -hf ggml-org/gemma-3-1b-it-GGUF"><pre>llama-cli -hf ggml-org/gemma-3-1b-it-GGUF</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">By default, the CLI would download from Hugging Face, you can switch to other options with the environment variable <code>MODEL_ENDPOINT</code>. The <code>MODEL_ENDPOINT</code> must point to a Hugging Face compatible API endpoint.</p>
<p dir="auto">After downloading a model, use the CLI tools to run it locally - see below.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>llama.cpp</code> requires the model to be stored in the <a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/blob/master/docs/gguf.md">GGUF</a> file format. Models in other data formats can be converted to GGUF using the <code>convert_*.py</code> Python scripts in this repo.</p>
<p dir="auto">The Hugging Face platform provides a variety of online tools for converting, quantizing and hosting models with <code>llama.cpp</code>:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Use the <a href="https://huggingface.co/spaces/ggml-org/gguf-my-repo" rel="nofollow">GGUF-my-repo space</a> to convert to GGUF format and quantize model weights to smaller sizes</li>
<li>Use the <a href="https://huggingface.co/spaces/ggml-org/gguf-my-lora" rel="nofollow">GGUF-my-LoRA space</a> to convert LoRA adapters to GGUF format (more info: <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="7406177" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/10123" data-hovercard-type="discussion" data-hovercard-url="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/10123/hovercard" href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/10123">#10123</a>)</li>
<li>Use the <a href="https://huggingface.co/spaces/CISCai/gguf-editor" rel="nofollow">GGUF-editor space</a> to edit GGUF meta data in the browser (more info: <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="7120529" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9268" data-hovercard-type="discussion" data-hovercard-url="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9268/hovercard" href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9268">#9268</a>)</li>
<li>Use the <a href="https://ui.endpoints.huggingface.co/" rel="nofollow">Inference Endpoints</a> to directly host <code>llama.cpp</code> in the cloud (more info: <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="7244750" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9669" data-hovercard-type="discussion" data-hovercard-url="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9669/hovercard" href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/9669">#9669</a>)</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">To learn more about model quantization, <a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/tools/quantize/README.md">read this documentation</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/tools/cli"><code>llama-cli</code></a></h2><a id="user-content-llama-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: llama-cli" href="#llama-cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">A CLI tool for accessing and experimenting with most of <code>llama.cpp</code>'s functionality.</h4><a id="user-content-a-cli-tool-for-accessing-and-experimenting-with-most-of-llamacpps-functionality" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: A CLI tool for accessing and experimenting with most of llama.cpp's functionality." href="#a-cli-tool-for-accessing-and-experimenting-with-most-of-llamacpps-functionality"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<details open="">
  <summary>Run in conversation mode</summary>
<p dir="auto">Models with a built-in chat template will automatically activate conversation mode. If this doesn't occur, you can manually enable it by adding <code>-cnv</code> and specifying a suitable chat template with <code>--chat-template NAME</code></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="llama-cli -m model.gguf

# &gt; hi, who are you?
# Hi there! I'm your helpful assistant! I'm an AI-powered chatbot designed to assist and provide information to users like you. I'm here to help answer your questions, provide guidance, and offer support on a wide range of topics. I'm a friendly and knowledgeable AI, and I'm always happy to help with anything you need. What's on your mind, and how can I assist you today?
#
# &gt; what is 1+1?
# Easy peasy! The answer to 1+1 is... 2!"><pre>llama-cli -m model.gguf

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> &gt; hi, who are you?</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Hi there! I'm your helpful assistant! I'm an AI-powered chatbot designed to assist and provide information to users like you. I'm here to help answer your questions, provide guidance, and offer support on a wide range of topics. I'm a friendly and knowledgeable AI, and I'm always happy to help with anything you need. What's on your mind, and how can I assist you today?</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span></span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> &gt; what is 1+1?</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Easy peasy! The answer to 1+1 is... 2!</span></pre></div>
  </details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
  <summary>Run in conversation mode with custom chat template</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# use the &quot;chatml&quot; template (use -h to see the list of supported templates)
llama-cli -m model.gguf -cnv --chat-template chatml

# use a custom template
llama-cli -m model.gguf -cnv --in-prefix 'User: ' --reverse-prompt 'User:'"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> use the "chatml" template (use -h to see the list of supported templates)</span>
llama-cli -m model.gguf -cnv --chat-template chatml

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> use a custom template</span>
llama-cli -m model.gguf -cnv --in-prefix <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>User: <span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> --reverse-prompt <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>User:<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span></pre></div>
  </details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
  <summary>Constrain the output with a custom grammar</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="llama-cli -m model.gguf -n 256 --grammar-file grammars/json.gbnf -p 'Request: schedule a call at 8pm; Command:'

# {&quot;appointmentTime&quot;: &quot;8pm&quot;, &quot;appointmentDetails&quot;: &quot;schedule a a call&quot;}"><pre>llama-cli -m model.gguf -n 256 --grammar-file grammars/json.gbnf -p <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>Request: schedule a call at 8pm; Command:<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> {"appointmentTime": "8pm", "appointmentDetails": "schedule a a call"}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The <a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/grammars">grammars/</a> folder contains a handful of sample grammars. To write your own, check out the <a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/grammars/README.md">GBNF Guide</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">For authoring more complex JSON grammars, check out <a href="https://grammar.intrinsiclabs.ai/" rel="nofollow">https://grammar.intrinsiclabs.ai/</a></p>
  </details>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/tools/server"><code>llama-server</code></a></h2><a id="user-content-llama-server" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: llama-server" href="#llama-server"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">A lightweight, <a href="https://github.com/openai/openai-openapi">OpenAI API</a> compatible, HTTP server for serving LLMs.</h4><a id="user-content-a-lightweight-openai-api-compatible-http-server-for-serving-llms" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: A lightweight, OpenAI API compatible, HTTP server for serving LLMs." href="#a-lightweight-openai-api-compatible-http-server-for-serving-llms"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<details open="">
  <summary>Start a local HTTP server with default configuration on port 8080</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="llama-server -m model.gguf --port 8080

# Basic web UI can be accessed via browser: http://localhost:8080
# Chat completion endpoint: http://localhost:8080/v1/chat/completions"><pre>llama-server -m model.gguf --port 8080

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Basic web UI can be accessed via browser: http://localhost:8080</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Chat completion endpoint: http://localhost:8080/v1/chat/completions</span></pre></div>
  </details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
  <summary>Support multiple-users and parallel decoding</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# up to 4 concurrent requests, each with 4096 max context
llama-server -m model.gguf -c 16384 -np 4"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> up to 4 concurrent requests, each with 4096 max context</span>
llama-server -m model.gguf -c 16384 -np 4</pre></div>
  </details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
  <summary>Enable speculative decoding</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# the draft.gguf model should be a small variant of the target model.gguf
llama-server -m model.gguf -md draft.gguf"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> the draft.gguf model should be a small variant of the target model.gguf</span>
llama-server -m model.gguf -md draft.gguf</pre></div>
  </details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
  <summary>Serve an embedding model</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# use the /embedding endpoint
llama-server -m model.gguf --embedding --pooling cls -ub 8192"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> use the /embedding endpoint</span>
llama-server -m model.gguf --embedding --pooling cls -ub 8192</pre></div>
  </details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
  <summary>Serve a reranking model</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# use the /reranking endpoint
llama-server -m model.gguf --reranking"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> use the /reranking endpoint</span>
llama-server -m model.gguf --reranking</pre></div>
  </details>
</li>
<li>
<details>
  <summary>Constrain all outputs with a grammar</summary>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# custom grammar
llama-server -m model.gguf --grammar-file grammar.gbnf

# JSON
llama-server -m model.gguf --grammar-file grammars/json.gbnf"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> custom grammar</span>
llama-server -m model.gguf --grammar-file grammar.gbnf

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> JSON</span>
llama-server -m model.gguf --grammar-file grammars/json.gbnf</pre></div>
  </details>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/tools/perplexity"><code>llama-perplexity</code></a></h2><a id="user-content-llama-perplexity" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: llama-perplexity" href="#llama-perplexity"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing</h2><a id="user-content-contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing" href="#contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Contributors can open PRs</li>
<li>Collaborators will be invited based on contributions</li>
<li>Maintainers can push to branches in the <code>llama.cpp</code> repo and merge PRs into the <code>master</code> branch</li>
<li>Any help with managing issues, PRs and projects is very appreciated!</li>
<li>See <a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/issues?q=is%3Aissue+is%3Aopen+label%3A%22good+first+issue%22">good first issues</a> for tasks suitable for first contributions</li>
<li>Read the <a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/CONTRIBUTING.md">CONTRIBUTING.md</a> for more information</li>
<li>Make sure to read this: <a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/205" data-hovercard-type="discussion" data-hovercard-url="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/205/hovercard">Inference at the edge</a></li>
<li>A bit of backstory for those who are interested: <a href="https://changelog.com/podcast/532" rel="nofollow">Changelog podcast</a></li>
</ul>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Development documentation</h4><a id="user-content-development-documentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Development documentation" href="#development-documentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/docs/build.md">How to build</a></li>
<li><a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/docs/docker.md">Running on Docker</a></li>
<li><a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/docs/android.md">Build on Android</a></li>
<li><a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/docs/multi-gpu.md">Multi-GPU usage</a></li>
<li><a href="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/blob/master/docs/development/token_generation_performance_tips.md">Performance troubleshooting</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/wiki/GGML-Tips-&amp;-Tricks">GGML tips &amp; tricks</a></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Seminal papers and background on the models</h4><a id="user-content-seminal-papers-and-background-on-the-models" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Seminal papers and background on the models" href="#seminal-papers-and-background-on-the-models"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If your issue is with model generation quality, then please at least scan the following links and papers to understand the limitations of LLaMA models. This is especially important when choosing an appropriate model size and appreciating both the significant and subtle differences between LLaMA models and ChatGPT:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>LLaMA:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://ai.facebook.com/blog/large-language-model-llama-meta-ai/" rel="nofollow">Introducing LLaMA: A foundational, 65-billion-parameter large language model</a></li>
<li><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.13971" rel="nofollow">LLaMA: Open and Efficient Foundation Language Models</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>GPT-3
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2005.14165" rel="nofollow">Language Models are Few-Shot Learners</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>GPT-3.5 / InstructGPT / ChatGPT:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://openai.com/research/instruction-following" rel="nofollow">Aligning language models to follow instructions</a></li>
<li><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.02155" rel="nofollow">Training language models to follow instructions with human feedback</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">XCFramework</h2><a id="user-content-xcframework" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: XCFramework" href="#xcframework"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The XCFramework is a precompiled version of the library for iOS, visionOS, tvOS,
and macOS. It can be used in Swift projects without the need to compile the
library from source. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-swift notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// swift-tools-version: 5.10
// The swift-tools-version declares the minimum version of Swift required to build this package.

import PackageDescription

let package = Package(
    name: &quot;MyLlamaPackage&quot;,
    targets: [
        .executableTarget(
            name: &quot;MyLlamaPackage&quot;,
            dependencies: [
                &quot;LlamaFramework&quot;
            ]),
        .binaryTarget(
            name: &quot;LlamaFramework&quot;,
            url: &quot;https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/releases/download/b5046/llama-b5046-xcframework.zip&quot;,
            checksum: &quot;c19be78b5f00d8d29a25da41042cb7afa094cbf6280a225abe614b03b20029ab&quot;
        )
    ]
)"><pre>// swift-tools-version: 5.10
// The swift-tools-version declares the minimum version of Swift required to build this package.

<span class="pl-k">import</span> PackageDescription

<span class="pl-k">let</span> <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-k">package</span></span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">Package</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span>
    name<span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-s">MyLlamaPackage</span><span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    targets<span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-kos">[</span>
        <span class="pl-kos">.</span>executableTarget<span class="pl-kos">(</span>
            name<span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-s">MyLlamaPackage</span><span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            dependencies<span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-kos">[</span>
                <span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-s">LlamaFramework</span><span class="pl-s">"</span>
            <span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
        <span class="pl-kos">.</span>binaryTarget<span class="pl-kos">(</span>
            name<span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-s">LlamaFramework</span><span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            url<span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-s">https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/releases/download/b5046/llama-b5046-xcframework.zip</span><span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
            checksum<span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-s">"</span><span class="pl-s">c19be78b5f00d8d29a25da41042cb7afa094cbf6280a225abe614b03b20029ab</span><span class="pl-s">"</span>
        <span class="pl-kos">)</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">]</span>
<span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The above example is using an intermediate build <code>b5046</code> of the library. This can be modified
to use a different version by changing the URL and checksum.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Completions</h2><a id="user-content-completions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Completions" href="#completions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Command-line completion is available for some environments.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Bash Completion</h4><a id="user-content-bash-completion" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Bash Completion" href="#bash-completion"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ build/bin/llama-cli --completion-bash &gt; ~/.llama-completion.bash
$ source ~/.llama-completion.bash"><pre>$ build/bin/llama-cli --completion-bash <span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> <span class="pl-k">~</span>/.llama-completion.bash
$ <span class="pl-c1">source</span> <span class="pl-k">~</span>/.llama-completion.bash</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Optionally this can be added to your <code>.bashrc</code> or <code>.bash_profile</code> to load it
automatically. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-shell-session notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ echo &quot;source ~/.llama-completion.bash&quot; &gt;&gt; ~/.bashrc"><pre>$ <span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-c1">echo</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>source ~/.llama-completion.bash<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">&gt;&gt;</span> <span class="pl-k">~</span>/.bashrc</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Dependencies</h2><a id="user-content-dependencies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Dependencies" href="#dependencies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<li><a href="https://github.com/yhirose/cpp-httplib">yhirose/cpp-httplib</a> - Single-header HTTP server, used by <code>llama-server</code> - MIT license</li>
